Shared by the console and Export.\"\"\"\n2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n3\t\n4\timport re\n5\timport sqlite3\n6\timport unicodedata\n7\tfrom datetime import date, time\n8\t\n9\tfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n10\t\n11\tKINDS = (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\")\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tclass PostError(ValueError):\n15\t    \"\"\"A problem with what the User typed, worded for the form.\"\"\"\n16\t\n17\t\n18\tdef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n19\t    \"\"\"Lower-case ASCII, accents stripped, hyphens between words.\"\"\"\n20\t    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n21\t    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, slug: str,\n25\t           except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n26\t    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE kind = ? AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n27\t                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n31\t              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n32\t    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n33\t    if typed.strip():\n34\t        slug = slugify(typed)\n35\t        if not slug:\n36\t            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n37\t        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n38\t            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n39\t        return slug\n40\t    base = slugify(title)\n41\t    if not base:\n42\t        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n43\t    slug, n = base, 1\n44\t    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n45\t        n += 1\n46\t        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n47\t    return slug\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n51\t    title = title.strip()\n52\t    if not title:\n53\t        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n54\t    return title\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef _parsed(value: str, parse, label: str):\n58\t    try:\n59\t        return parse(value)\n60\t    except ValueError:\n61\t        raise PostError(f\"The {label} isn’t valid.\") from None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tdef _clean_event(kind: str, start_date: str, start_time: str,\n65\t                 end_date: str) -> tuple[str | None, str | None, str | None]:\n66\t    \"\"\"The Event date fields as stored. Only Events have them, and Events need a start date.\"\"\"\n67\t    typed = [v.strip() for v in (start_date, start_time, end_date)]\n68\t    if kind != \"event\":\n69\t        if any(typed):\n70\t            raise PostError(\"Event dates only apply to Events.\")\n71\t        return None, None, None\n72\t    start_date, start_time, end_date = typed\n73\t    if not start_date:\n74\t        raise PostError(\"An Event needs a start date.\")\n75\t    start = _parsed(start_date, date.fromisoformat, \"start date\")\n76\t    at = _parsed(start_time, time.fromisoformat, \"start time\") if start_time else None\n77\t    end = _parsed(end_date, date.fromisoformat, \"end date\") if end_date else None\n78\t    if end and end < start:\n79\t        raise PostError(\"The end date can’t be earlier than the start date.\")\n80\t    return (start.isoformat(), at.strftime(\"%H:%M\") if at else None,\n81\t            end.isoformat() if end else None)\n82\t\n83\t\n84\tdef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n85\t                body_md: str, author_id: int, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n86\t                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> int:\n87\t    if kind not in KINDS:\n88\t        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n89\t    title = _clean_title(title)\n90\t    event = _clean_event(kind, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n91\t    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n92\t    now = utc_now()\n93\t    cur = conn.execute(\n94\t        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n95\t        \"updated_at, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date) \"\n96\t        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n97\t        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now, *event))\n98\t    conn.commit()\n99\t    return cur.lastrowid\n100\t\n101\t\n102\tdef clean_update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n103\t                 slug: str, event_start_date: str = \"\", event_start_time: str = \"\",\n104\t                 event_end_date: str = \"\") -> dict:\n105\t    \"\"\"The editable values as they would be stored, or a PostError. Changes nothing.\n106\t\n107\t    Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\n108\t    \"\"\"\n109\t    title = _clean_title(title)\n110\t    event = _clean_event(post[\"kind\"], event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n111\t    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n112\t        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n113\t    else:\n114\t        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n115\t    return {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"event_start_date\": event[0],\n116\t            \"event_start_time\": event[1], \"event_end_date\": event[2]}\n117\t\n118\t\n119\tdef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n120\t                slug: str, body_md: str, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n121\t                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> None:\n122\t    v = clean_update(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, event_start_date=event_start_date,\n123\t                     event_start_time=event_start_time, event_end_date=event_end_date)\n124\t    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ?, \"\n125\t                 \"event_start_date = ?, event_start_time = ?, event_end_date = ? \"\n126\t                 \"WHERE id = ?\",\n127\t                 (v[\"title\"], v[\"slug\"], body_md, utc_now(), v[\"event_start_date\"],\n128\t                  v[\"event_start_time\"], v[\"event_end_date\"], post[\"id\"]))\n129\t    conn.commit()\n130\t\n131\t\n132\tdef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n133\t    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n134\t\n135\t\n136\tdef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n137\t    return conn.execute(\n138\t        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n139\t        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n140\t        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\n141\t\n142\t\n143\tdef publish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n144\t    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n145\t    now = utc_now()\n146\t    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n147\t                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n148\t                 (now, now, post[\"id\"]))\n149\t    conn.commit()\n150\t\n151\t\n152\tdef unpublish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n153\t    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n154\t    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n155\t                 (utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n156\t    conn.commit()\n157\t\n158\t\n159\tdef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n160\t    \"\"\"Permanently remove a Post. A Published one is remembered, so the\n161\t    not-yet-Live count includes it until the next Export.\"\"\"\n162\t    if post[\"status\"] == \"published\":\n163\t        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO deleted_published (deleted_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n164\t    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post[\"id\"],))\n165\t    conn.commit()\n166\t\n167\t\n168\tdef changes_not_live(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:\n169\t    \"\"\"Changes Members can't see yet: Posts touched since the last Export, plus\n170\t    Live Posts deleted since then. Before any Export, every Published Post counts.\"\"\"\n171\t    last = conn.execute(\"SELECT max(ran_at) FROM exports\").fetchone()[0]\n172\t    if last is None:\n173\t        return conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE status = 'published'\"\n174\t                            ).fetchone()[0]\n175\t    touched = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE updated_at > ?\",\n176\t                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n177\t    deleted = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM deleted_published WHERE deleted_at > ?\",\n178\t                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n179\t    return touched + deleted\n180\t\n181\t\n182\tdef published_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str | None = None,\n183\t                    limit: int | None = None) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n184\t    \"\"\"Published Posts, newest first. Selects no Author: the public site has none.\"\"\"\n185\t    sql = (\"SELECT id, kind, title, slug, body_md, first_published_at, \"\n186\t           \"event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date FROM posts \"\n187\t           \"WHERE status = 'published'\")\n188\t    args: list = []\n189\t    if kind:\n190\t        sql += \" AND kind = ?\"\n191\t        args.append(kind)\n192\t    sql += \" ORDER BY first_published_at DESC, id DESC\"\n193\t    if limit:\n194\t        sql += \" LIMIT ?\"\n195\t        args.append(limit)\n196\t    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchall()\n197\t\n198\t\n199\tdef published_events(conn: sqlite3.Connection,\n200\t                     export_date: date) -> tuple[list[sqlite3.Row], list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n201\t    \"\"\"(Upcoming, past) Published Events.\n202\t\n203\t    Upcoming: the end date, or the start date if there is none, is on or after\n204\t    the export date. Upcoming is soonest first; past is most recent first.\n205\t    \"\"\"\n206\t    today = export_date.isoformat()\n207\t\n208\t    def last_day(event) -> str:\n209\t        return event[\"event_end_date\"] or event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\"\n210\t\n211\t    def starts(event) -> tuple:\n212\t        return (event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\", event[\"event_start_time\"] or \"\",\n213\t                event[\"id\"])\n214\t\n215\t    events = published_posts(conn, \"event\")\n216\t    upcoming = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) >= today), key=starts)\n217\t    past = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) < today),\n218\t                  key=lambda e: (last_day(e), *starts(e)), reverse=True)\n219\t    return upcoming, past\n220\t\n221\t\n222\tdef published_menus(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n223\t    \"\"\"(Current menu, Menu archive): the newest Published Menu, then all the others.\"\"\"\n224\t    menus = published_posts(conn, \"menu\")\n225\t    return (menus[0] if menus else None), menus[1:]\n226\t\"\"\"Post list, editor and Preview. Editors and Admins can do all of it.\"\"\"\n227\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n228\t\n229\timport sqlite3\n230\t\n231\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\n232\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n233\t\n234\tfrom app import auth, content\n235\tfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\n236\tfrom app.timeutil import fecha_evento\n237\tfrom app.web import templates\n238\t\n239\trouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/posts\")\n240\t\n241\tKIND_LABELS = {\"news\": \"News\", \"event\": \"Event\", \"menu\": \"Menu\"}\n242\tKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n243\t\n244\t\n245\tdef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: dict, *,\n246\t              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n247\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n248\t        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n249\t        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post.get(\"id\") else \"New post\", \"user\": user,\n250\t         \"post\": post, \"error\": error,\n251\t         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS},\n252\t        status_code=status_code)\n253\t\n254\t\n255\tdef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n256\t    post = content.get_post(conn, post_id)\n257\t    if post is None:\n258\t        raise HTTPException(404, \"Post not found\")\n259\t    return post\n260\t\n261\t\n262\tEDITOR_FIELDS = (\"title\", \"slug\", \"body_md\", \"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\",\n263\t                 \"event_end_date\")\n264\t\n265\t\n266\tasync def pending_values(request: Request) -> dict | None:\n267\t    \"\"\"The editor's values as submitted, or None if the form carried none.\n268\t\n269\t    Read from the raw form: FastAPI turns an empty optional Form field into\n270\t    None, which would make a blank title look like \"no values sent\".\n271\t    \"\"\"\n272\t    form = await request.form()\n273\t    if \"title\" not in form:\n274\t        return None\n275\t    return {name: str(form.get(name) or \"\") for name in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n276\t\n277\t\n278\tdef clean(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, values: dict) -> dict:\n279\t    \"\"\"Validate the editor's values (everything but the body) without saving.\"\"\"\n280\t    return content.clean_update(conn, post, **{k: v for k, v in values.items()\n281\t                                               if k != \"body_md\"})\n282\t\n283\t\n284\tdef article_context(post: dict) -> dict:\n285\t    \"\"\"What public/_article.html needs to show a Post as Members will see it.\"\"\"\n286\t    return {\"post\": post, \"date_label\": None, \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n287\t            \"event_label\": fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n288\t                                        post[\"event_end_date\"]),\n289\t            \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])}\n290\t\n291\t\n292\tdef changes(post: sqlite3.Row, cleaned: dict, body_md: str) -> list[str]:\n293\t    \"\"\"Plain-language list of what saving would change.\"\"\"\n294\t    labels = {\"title\": \"Title\", \"slug\": \"Slug\", \"event_start_date\": \"Start date\",\n295\t              \"event_start_time\": \"Start time\", \"event_end_date\": \"End date\"}\n296\t    lines = [f\"{label}: “{post[key] or ''}” → “{cleaned[key] or ''}”\"\n297\t             for key, label in labels.items() if (post[key] or \"\") != (cleaned[key] or \"\")]\n298\t    if post[\"body_md\"] != body_md:\n299\t        lines.append(\"Body: changed (see the preview below)\")\n300\t    return lines or [\"No changes.\"]\n301\t\n302\t\n303\tdef confirmation(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: sqlite3.Row, *, action: str,\n304\t                 heading: str, summary: list[str], pending: dict | None,\n305\t                 button: str, preview: dict | None = None):\n306\t    \"\"\"The Confirmation screen: nothing has been changed yet.\n307\t\n308\t    It carries the pending values in hidden fields. Only a second POST with the\n309\t    confirm flag applies the change; Cancel reopens the editor with the values.\n310\t    \"\"\"\n311\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n312\t        request, \"admin/confirm.html\",\n313\t        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"summary\": summary,\n314\t         \"pending\": pending or {}, \"action\": action, \"button\": button,\n315\t         \"preview\": preview and article_context(preview)})\n316\t\n317\t\n318\t@router.get(\"\")\n319\tdef post_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n320\t              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n321\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n322\t        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n323\t        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": content.list_posts(conn),\n324\t         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS})\n325\t\n326\t\n327\t@router.get(\"/new\")\n328\tdef new_post(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n329\t    return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\",\n330\t                                     \"body_md\": \"\", \"event_start_date\": \"\",\n331\t                                     \"event_start_time\": \"\", \"event_end_date\": \"\"})\n332\t\n333\t\n334\t@router.post(\"\")\n335\tdef create_post(request: Request, kind: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n336\t                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n337\t                event_start_date: str = Form(\"\"), event_start_time: str = Form(\"\"),\n338\t                event_end_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n339\t                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n340\t                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n341\t    event = dict(event_start_date=event_start_date, event_start_time=event_start_time,\n342\t                 event_end_date=event_end_date)\n343\t    try:\n344\t        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n345\t                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"], **event)\n346\t    except content.PostError as exc:\n347\t        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n348\t                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, **event},\n349\t                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n350\t    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n351\t\n352\t\n353\t@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\n354\tdef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n355\t              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n356\t              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n357\t    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n358\t\n359\t\n360\tdef editor_page(request, user, post: sqlite3.Row, values: dict | None, error: str,\n361\t                status_code: int = 422):\n362\t    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), **(values or {})}, error=error,\n363\t                     status_code=status_code)\n364\t\n365\t\n366\t@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\n367\tdef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n368\t              values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n369\t              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n370\t              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n371\t    post = load(conn, post_id)\n372\t    values = values or {k: \"\" for k in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n373\t    try:\n374\t        cleaned = clean(conn, post, values)\n375\t        if post[\"status\"] == \"published\" and not confirm:\n376\t            return confirmation(\n377\t                request, user, post, action=\"save_post\", button=\"Save changes\",\n378\t                heading=\"Confirm changes to Published post\", pending=values,\n379\t                summary=[\"This post is Published. Saving changes what Members see \"\n380\t                         \"at the next Export.\", *changes(post, cleaned, values[\"body_md\"])],\n381\t                preview={**dict(post), **cleaned, \"body_md\": values[\"body_md\"]})\n382\t        content.update_post(conn, post, **values)\n383\t    except content.PostError as exc:\n384\t        return editor_page(request, user, post, values, str(exc))\n385\t    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n386\t\n387\t\n388\t@router.post(\"/{post_id}/edit\")\n389\tdef back_to_editor(request: Request, post_id: int,\n390\t                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n391\t                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n392\t                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n393\t    \"\"\"Cancel on a Confirmation screen: the editor again, with the unsaved values.\"\"\"\n394\t    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(load(conn, post_id)), **(values or {})})\n395\t\n396\t\n397\t@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\n398\tdef publish_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n399\t                 values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n400\t                 user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n401\t                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n402\t    post = load(conn, post_id)\n403\t    try:\n404\t        if values is not None:  # what's on the editor is what gets published\n405\t            cleaned = clean(conn, post, values)\n406\t        if not confirm:\n407\t            shown = {**dict(post), **(cleaned if values is not None else {}),\n408\t                     **({\"body_md\": values[\"body_md\"]} if values is not None else {})}\n409\t            return confirmation(\n410\t                request, user, post, action=\"publish_post\", button=\"Publish\",\n411\t                heading=\"Confirm publishing\", pending=values, preview=shown,\n412\t                summary=[f\"Publish “{shown['title']}”? It stays a Draft until you confirm, \"\n413\t                         \"and reaches the public site at the next Export.\"])\n414\t        if values is not None:\n415\t            content.update_post(conn, post, **values)\n416\t            post = load(conn, post_id)\n417\t    except content.PostError as exc:\n418\t        return editor_page(request, user, post, values, str(exc))\n419\t    content.publish_post(conn, post)\n420\t    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n421\t\n422\t\n423\t@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\n424\tdef unpublish_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n425\t                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n426\t                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n427\t                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n428\t    post = load(conn, post_id)\n429\t    if not confirm:\n430\t        return confirmation(\n431\t            request, user, post, action=\"unpublish_post\", button=\"Unpublish\",\n432\t            heading=\"Confirm unpublishing\", pending=values,\n433\t            summary=[f\"Unpublish “{post['title']}”? It returns to Draft and disappears \"\n434\t                     \"from the public site at the next Export. Unsaved edits in the \"\n435\t                     \"editor are not saved.\"])\n436\t    content.unpublish_post(conn, post)\n437\t    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n438\t\n439\t\n440\t@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\n441\tdef delete_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n442\t                values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n443\t                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n444\t                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n445\t    post = load(conn, post_id)\n446\t    if not confirm:\n447\t        return confirmation(\n448\t            request, user, post, action=\"delete_post\", button=\"Delete permanently\",\n449\t            heading=\"Confirm deleting\", pending=values,\n450\t            summary=[f\"Delete “{post['title']}”? Deletion is permanent: the post \"\n451\t                     \"can’t be recovered.\"]\n452\t            + ([\"It leaves the public site at the next Export.\"]\n453\t               if post[\"first_published_at\"] else []))\n454\t    content.delete_post(conn, post)\n455\t    return RedirectResponse(request.app.url_path_for(\"post_list\"), status_code=303)\n456\t\n457\t\n458\t@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")\n459\tdef preview(request: Request, post_id: int,\n460\t            conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n461\t    post = load(conn, post_id)\n462\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n463\t        request, \"public/post.html\",\n464\t        {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n465\t         \"home_path\": \"/\", **article_context(dict(post))})\n466\t\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n467\t\n468\tThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\n469\tforget it. Pages and Users are Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07.\n470\t\"\"\"\n471\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n472\t\n473\timport sqlite3\n474\t\n475\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n476\t\n477\tfrom app import auth, content\n478\tfrom app.routes import posts\n479\tfrom app.web import templates\n480\t\n481\trouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n482\t# Before the catch-all 404 at the bottom of this file.\n483\trouter.include_router(posts.router)\n484\t\n485\t\n486\tdef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str, **ctx):\n487\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n488\t                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user, **ctx})\n489\t\n490\t\n491\t@router.get(\"\")\n492\tdef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n493\t              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n494\t    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\",\n495\t                not_live=content.changes_not_live(conn))\n496\t\n497\t\n498\t@router.get(\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\n499\tdef pages(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n500\t    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Pages\")\n501\t\n502\t\n503\t@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\n504\tdef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n505\t    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n506\t\n507\t\n508\t@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\n509\tdef not_found(path: str):\n510\t    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n511\t    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\n512\t\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n513\t\n514\tTwo rules the rubric checks:\n515\t\n516\t  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n517\t  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n518\t     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n519\t\"\"\"\n520\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n521\t\n522\timport shutil\n523\tfrom datetime import date\n524\tfrom pathlib import Path\n525\t\n526\tfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n527\t\n528\tfrom app import content, db, settings\n529\tfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\n530\tfrom app.timeutil import fecha, fecha_evento, today_lima, utc_now\n531\t\n532\tFOLDERS = {\"news\": \"noticias\", \"event\": \"eventos\", \"menu\": \"menus\"}\n533\tKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n534\tHOME_NEWS = 5\n535\tHOME_EVENTS = 3\n536\t\n537\tCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n538\t:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\n539\tbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\n540\theader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\n541\tmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n542\t\"\"\"\n543\t\n544\t\n545\tdef environment() -> Environment:\n546\t    return Environment(\n547\t        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n548\t        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n549\t    )\n550\t\n551\t\n552\tdef _write(out: Path, name: str, html: str) -> None:\n553\t    path = out / name\n554\t    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n555\t    path.write_text(html)\n556\t\n557\t\n558\tdef _event_label(post) -> str:\n559\t    return fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n560\t                        post[\"event_end_date\"])\n561\t\n562\t\n563\tdef _date_label(post) -> str:\n564\t    \"\"\"Events are dated by when they happen; News and Menus by first publish.\"\"\"\n565\t    if post[\"kind\"] == \"event\":\n566\t        return _event_label(post)\n567\t    return fecha(post[\"first_published_at\"])\n568\t\n569\t\n570\tdef _link(post, root: str) -> dict:\n571\t    \"\"\"A feed entry. `root` is the relative path from the page back to site/.\"\"\"\n572\t    return {\"title\": post[\"title\"],\n573\t            \"href\": f\"{root}{FOLDERS[post['kind']]}/{post['slug']}.html\",\n574\t            \"date\": _date_label(post)}\n575\t\n576\t\n577\tdef render_site(out: Path | None = None, export_date: date | None = None) -> Path:\n578\t    \"\"\"Write every Published Post into `out` and record the Export.\n579\t\n580\t    `export_date` (a Lima date) is what Upcoming is measured against from T04 on.\n581\t    \"\"\"\n582\t    out = out or settings.SITE\n583\t    export_date = export_date or today_lima()\n584\t    env = environment()\n585\t    db.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)  # an older database may lack `exports`\n586\t    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n587\t    try:\n588\t        posts = content.published_posts(conn)\n589\t        upcoming, past = content.published_events(conn, export_date)\n590\t        current_menu, archive = content.published_menus(conn)\n591\t    finally:\n592\t        conn.close()\n593\t\n594\t    if out.exists():\n595\t        shutil.rmtree(out)\n596\t    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n597\t    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n598\t\n599\t    def page(template: str, depth: int, **ctx) -> str:\n600\t        root = \"../\" * depth\n601\t        nav = [(\"Inicio\", f\"{root}index.html\"),\n602\t               (\"Noticias\", f\"{root}noticias/index.html\"),\n603\t               (\"Eventos\", f\"{root}eventos/index.html\"),\n604\t               (\"Menús anteriores\", f\"{root}menus/index.html\")]\n605\t        return env.get_template(template).render(\n606\t            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, lang=\"es\", nav=nav, css_path=f\"{root}style.css\",\n607\t            home_path=f\"{root}index.html\", **ctx)\n608\t\n609\t    news = [p for p in posts if p[\"kind\"] == \"news\"]\n610\t    menu = None\n611\t    if current_menu:\n612\t        menu = {**_link(current_menu, \"\"),\n613\t                \"body_html\": render_markdown(current_menu[\"body_md\"])}\n614\t    _write(out, \"index.html\", page(\n615\t        \"public/home.html\", 0, menu=menu,\n616\t        events=[_link(p, \"\") for p in upcoming[:HOME_EVENTS]],\n617\t        news=[_link(p, \"\") for p in news[:HOME_NEWS]]))\n618\t    _write(out, \"noticias/index.html\", page(\n619\t        \"public/feed.html\", 1, page_title=\"Noticias\", heading=\"Noticias\",\n620\t        items=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in news]))\n621\t    _write(out, \"eventos/index.html\", page(\n622\t        \"public/events.html\", 1, page_title=\"Eventos\",\n623\t        upcoming=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in upcoming],\n624\t        past=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in past]))\n625\t    _write(out, \"menus/index.html\", page(\n626\t        \"public/feed.html\", 1, page_title=\"Menús anteriores\",\n627\t        heading=\"Menús anteriores\", items=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in archive]))\n628\t    for p in posts:\n629\t        event = p[\"kind\"] == \"event\"\n630\t        _write(out, f\"{FOLDERS[p['kind']]}/{p['slug']}.html\", page(\n631\t            \"public/post.html\", 1, page_title=p[\"title\"], post=p,\n632\t            kind_label=KIND_LABELS_ES[p[\"kind\"]],\n633\t            event_label=_event_label(p) if event else \"\",\n634\t            date_label=\"\" if event else fecha(p[\"first_published_at\"]),\n635\t            body_html=render_markdown(p[\"body_md\"])))\n636\t\n637\t    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n638\t    try:\n639\t        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO exports (ran_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n640\t        conn.commit()\n641\t    finally:\n642\t        conn.close()\n643\t    return out\n644\t\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n645\t\n646\tThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\n647\tanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\n648\tthem here. Keep this file small.\n649\t\"\"\"\n650\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n651\t\n652\tfrom pathlib import Path\n653\t\n654\tfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\n655\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse, Response\n656\tfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n657\t\n658\tfrom app import auth, db, publish, settings\n659\tfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\n660\tfrom app.routes import console\n661\tfrom app.web import templates\n662\t\n663\t\n664\tdef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n665\t    db_path = Path(database_path) if database_path else settings.DATABASE_PATH\n666\t    db.init_db(db_path)\n667\t\n668\t    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n669\t                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n670\t    app.state.db_path = db_path\n671\t    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n672\t                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n673\t                       max_age=8 * 60 * 60)\n674\t\n675\t    @app.exception_handler(auth.LoginRequired)\n676\t    def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.LoginRequired):\n677\t        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n678\t\n679\t    @app.get(\"/style.css\")\n680\t    def stylesheet():\n681\t        # Lets the console's Preview load the public stylesheet.\n682\t        return Response(publish.CSS, media_type=\"text/css\")\n683\t\n684\t    @app.get(\"/\")\n685\t    def public_home(request: Request):\n686\t        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n687\t            request, \"public/home.html\",\n688\t            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"news\": []},\n689\t        )\n690\t\n691\t    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n692\t    app.include_router(console.router)\n693\t    return app\n694\t\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\n695\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n696\t\n697\timport sqlite3\n698\tfrom pathlib import Path\n699\t\n700\tSCHEMA = \"\"\"\n701\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n702\t    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n703\t    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n704\t    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n705\t    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n706\t    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n707\t    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n708\t    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n709\t    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n710\t);\n711\t\n712\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n713\t    id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n714\t    kind              TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('news', 'event', 'menu')),\n715\t    title             TEXT    NOT NULL,\n716\t    slug              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n717\t    body_md           TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n718\t    status            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n719\t                              CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n720\t    author_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n721\t    created_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n722\t    updated_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n723\t    first_published_at TEXT,\n724\t    event_start_date  TEXT,\n725\t    event_start_time  TEXT,\n726\t    event_end_date    TEXT,\n727\t    UNIQUE (kind, slug)\n728\t);\n729\t\n730\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (\n731\t    id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n732\t    ran_at TEXT NOT NULL\n733\t);\n734\t\n735\t-- One row per Post that had been Published and was then deleted. Counted as a\n736\t-- change not yet Live until the next Export.\n737\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deleted_published (\n738\t    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n739\t    deleted_at TEXT NOT NULL\n740\t);\n741\t\"\"\"\n742\t\n743\t\n744\tdef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n745\t    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n746\t    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n747\t    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n748\t    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n749\t    return conn\n750\t\n751\t\n752\tEVENT_COLUMNS = (\"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\", \"event_end_date\")\n753\t\n754\t\n755\tdef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n756\t    conn = connect(path)\n757\t    try:\n758\t        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n759\t        # A database made before T04 has no Event date columns.\n760\t        have = {row[\"name\"] for row in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(posts)\")}\n761\t        for column in EVENT_COLUMNS:\n762\t            if column not in have:\n763\t                conn.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE posts ADD COLUMN {column} TEXT\")\n764\t        conn.commit()\n765\t    finally:\n766\t        conn.close()\n767\t\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\n768\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n769\t\n770\timport jinja2\n771\tfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n772\t\n773\tfrom app import settings\n774\tfrom app.auth import csrf_token\n775\tfrom app.timeutil import lima\n776\t\n777\ttemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n778\ttemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\n779\ttemplates.env.filters[\"lima\"] = lima\n780\t\n781\t\n782\t@jinja2.pass_context\n783\tdef path_for(context, name: str, **params) -> str:\n784\t    \"\"\"The path of a named console route, e.g. path_for('edit_post', post_id=3).\n785\t\n786\t    Unlike Starlette's url_for it stays a path (\"/admin/posts/3\"), not a full URL.\n787\t    \"\"\"\n788\t    return context[\"request\"].app.url_path_for(name, **params)\n789\t\n790\t\n791\ttemplates.env.globals[\"path_for\"] = path_for\n792\t\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n793\t\n794\t- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n795\t- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n796\t  Deactivated User on every request.\n797\t- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n798\t\"\"\"\n799\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n800\t\n801\timport hmac\n802\timport secrets\n803\timport sqlite3\n804\tfrom typing import Iterator\n805\t\n806\tfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n807\t\n808\tfrom app import accounts, db\n809\t\n810\tSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n811\t\n812\t\n813\tclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n814\t    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n815\t\n816\t\n817\tdef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n818\t    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n819\t    try:\n820\t        yield conn\n821\t    finally:\n822\t        conn.close()\n823\t\n824\t\n825\tdef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n826\t    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n827\t    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n828\t    if not token:\n829\t        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n830\t    return token\n831\t\n832\t\n833\tasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n834\t    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n835\t        return\n836\t    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n837\t    if sent is None:\n838\t        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n839\t    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n840\t    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n841\t            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n842\t        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n843\t\n844\t\n845\tdef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n846\t    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n847\t    request.session.clear()\n848\t    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n849\t    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n850\t    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n851\t\n852\t\n853\tdef current_user(request: Request,\n854\t                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n855\t    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n856\t    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n857\t    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n858\t            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n859\t        request.session.clear()\n860\t        raise LoginRequired()\n861\t    return user\n862\t\n863\t\n864\tdef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n865\t    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n866\t        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n867\t    return user\n868\t"}]},"uuid":"a4820cab-e5bc-4d6c-b05a-f120282938b2","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:10:37.545Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/.claude/projects/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/6849c5d9-d2dc-4a6e-802c-433f2aa1aa8f/tool-results/bm5u8nmtt.txt","content":"\"\"\"Posts: creation, editing, Slugs and the list. Shared by the console and Export.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom datetime import date, time\n\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nKINDS = (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\")\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem with what the User typed, worded for the form.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Lower-case ASCII, accents stripped, hyphens between words.\"\"\"\n    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, slug: str,\n           except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE kind = ? AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef _parsed(value: str, parse, label: str):\n    try:\n        return parse(value)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise PostError(f\"The {label} isn’t valid.\") from None\n\n\ndef _clean_event(kind: str, start_date: str, start_time: str,\n                 end_date: str) -> tuple[str | None, str | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"The Event date fields as stored. Only Events have them, and Events need a start date.\"\"\"\n    typed = [v.strip() for v in (start_date, start_time, end_date)]\n    if kind != \"event\":\n        if any(typed):\n            raise PostError(\"Event dates only apply to Events.\")\n        return None, None, None\n    start_date, start_time, end_date = typed\n    if not start_date:\n        raise PostError(\"An Event needs a start date.\")\n    start = _parsed(start_date, date.fromisoformat, \"start date\")\n    at = _parsed(start_time, time.fromisoformat, \"start time\") if start_time else None\n    end = _parsed(end_date, date.fromisoformat, \"end date\") if end_date else None\n    if end and end < start:\n        raise PostError(\"The end date can’t be earlier than the start date.\")\n    return (start.isoformat(), at.strftime(\"%H:%M\") if at else None,\n            end.isoformat() if end else None)\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, author_id: int, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> int:\n    if kind not in KINDS:\n        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(kind, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at, event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now, *event))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef clean_update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                 slug: str, event_start_date: str = \"\", event_start_time: str = \"\",\n                 event_end_date: str = \"\") -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The editable values as they would be stored, or a PostError. Changes nothing.\n\n    Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\n    \"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    event = _clean_event(post[\"kind\"], event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    return {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"event_start_date\": event[0],\n            \"event_start_time\": event[1], \"event_end_date\": event[2]}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str, event_start_date: str = \"\",\n                event_start_time: str = \"\", event_end_date: str = \"\") -> None:\n    v = clean_update(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, event_start_date=event_start_date,\n                     event_start_time=event_start_time, event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"event_start_date = ?, event_start_time = ?, event_end_date = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (v[\"title\"], v[\"slug\"], body_md, utc_now(), v[\"event_start_date\"],\n                  v[\"event_start_time\"], v[\"event_end_date\"], post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef publish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n    now = utc_now()\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (now, now, post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef unpublish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Permanently remove a Post. A Published one is remembered, so the\n    not-yet-Live count includes it until the next Export.\"\"\"\n    if post[\"status\"] == \"published\":\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO deleted_published (deleted_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post[\"id\"],))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef changes_not_live(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Changes Members can't see yet: Posts touched since the last Export, plus\n    Live Posts deleted since then. Before any Export, every Published Post counts.\"\"\"\n    last = conn.execute(\"SELECT max(ran_at) FROM exports\").fetchone()[0]\n    if last is None:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE status = 'published'\"\n                            ).fetchone()[0]\n    touched = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE updated_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n    deleted = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM deleted_published WHERE deleted_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]\n    return touched + deleted\n\n\ndef published_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str | None = None,\n                    limit: int | None = None) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Published Posts, newest first. Selects no Author: the public site has none.\"\"\"\n    sql = (\"SELECT id, kind, title, slug, body_md, first_published_at, \"\n           \"event_start_date, event_start_time, event_end_date FROM posts \"\n           \"WHERE status = 'published'\")\n    args: list = []\n    if kind:\n        sql += \" AND kind = ?\"\n        args.append(kind)\n    sql += \" ORDER BY first_published_at DESC, id DESC\"\n    if limit:\n        sql += \" LIMIT ?\"\n        args.append(limit)\n    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchall()\n\n\ndef published_events(conn: sqlite3.Connection,\n                     export_date: date) -> tuple[list[sqlite3.Row], list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n    \"\"\"(Upcoming, past) Published Events.\n\n    Upcoming: the end date, or the start date if there is none, is on or after\n    the export date. Upcoming is soonest first; past is most recent first.\n    \"\"\"\n    today = export_date.isoformat()\n\n    def last_day(event) -> str:\n        return event[\"event_end_date\"] or event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\"\n\n    def starts(event) -> tuple:\n        return (event[\"event_start_date\"] or \"\", event[\"event_start_time\"] or \"\",\n                event[\"id\"])\n\n    events = published_posts(conn, \"event\")\n    upcoming = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) >= today), key=starts)\n    past = sorted((e for e in events if last_day(e) < today),\n                  key=lambda e: (last_day(e), *starts(e)), reverse=True)\n    return upcoming, past\n\n\ndef published_menus(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, list[sqlite3.Row]]:\n    \"\"\"(Current menu, Menu archive): the newest Published Menu, then all the others.\"\"\"\n    menus = published_posts(conn, \"menu\")\n    return (menus[0] if menus else None), menus[1:]\n\"\"\"Post list, editor and Preview. Editors and Admins can do all of it.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.timeutil import fecha_evento\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/posts\")\n\nKIND_LABELS = {\"news\": \"News\", \"event\": \"Event\", \"menu\": \"Menu\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post.get(\"id\") else \"New post\", \"user\": user,\n         \"post\": post, \"error\": error,\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    post = content.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Post not found\")\n    return post\n\n\nEDITOR_FIELDS = (\"title\", \"slug\", \"body_md\", \"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\",\n                 \"event_end_date\")\n\n\nasync def pending_values(request: Request) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"The editor's values as submitted, or None if the form carried none.\n\n    Read from the raw form: FastAPI turns an empty optional Form field into\n    None, which would make a blank title look like \"no values sent\".\n    \"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    if \"title\" not in form:\n        return None\n    return {name: str(form.get(name) or \"\") for name in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n\n\ndef clean(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, values: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Validate the editor's values (everything but the body) without saving.\"\"\"\n    return content.clean_update(conn, post, **{k: v for k, v in values.items()\n                                               if k != \"body_md\"})\n\n\ndef article_context(post: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"What public/_article.html needs to show a Post as Members will see it.\"\"\"\n    return {\"post\": post, \"date_label\": None, \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n            \"event_label\": fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n                                        post[\"event_end_date\"]),\n            \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])}\n\n\ndef changes(post: sqlite3.Row, cleaned: dict, body_md: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Plain-language list of what saving would change.\"\"\"\n    labels = {\"title\": \"Title\", \"slug\": \"Slug\", \"event_start_date\": \"Start date\",\n              \"event_start_time\": \"Start time\", \"event_end_date\": \"End date\"}\n    lines = [f\"{label}: “{post[key] or ''}” → “{cleaned[key] or ''}”\"\n             for key, label in labels.items() if (post[key] or \"\") != (cleaned[key] or \"\")]\n    if post[\"body_md\"] != body_md:\n        lines.append(\"Body: changed (see the preview below)\")\n    return lines or [\"No changes.\"]\n\n\ndef confirmation(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: sqlite3.Row, *, action: str,\n                 heading: str, summary: list[str], pending: dict | None,\n                 button: str, preview: dict | None = None):\n    \"\"\"The Confirmation screen: nothing has been changed yet.\n\n    It carries the pending values in hidden fields. Only a second POST with the\n    confirm flag applies the change; Cancel reopens the editor with the values.\n    \"\"\"\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"summary\": summary,\n         \"pending\": pending or {}, \"action\": action, \"button\": button,\n         \"preview\": preview and article_context(preview)})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": content.list_posts(conn),\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_post(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\",\n                                     \"body_md\": \"\", \"event_start_date\": \"\",\n                                     \"event_start_time\": \"\", \"event_end_date\": \"\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_post(request: Request, kind: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n                event_start_date: str = Form(\"\"), event_start_time: str = Form(\"\"),\n                event_end_date: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    event = dict(event_start_date=event_start_date, event_start_time=event_start_time,\n                 event_end_date=event_end_date)\n    try:\n        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"], **event)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, **event},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n\n\ndef editor_page(request, user, post: sqlite3.Row, values: dict | None, error: str,\n                status_code: int = 422):\n    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), **(values or {})}, error=error,\n                     status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n              values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    values = values or {k: \"\" for k in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n    try:\n        cleaned = clean(conn, post, values)\n        if post[\"status\"] == \"published\" and not confirm:\n            return confirmation(\n                request, user, post, action=\"save_post\", button=\"Save changes\",\n                heading=\"Confirm changes to Published post\", pending=values,\n                summary=[\"This post is Published. Saving changes what Members see \"\n                         \"at the next Export.\", *changes(post, cleaned, values[\"body_md\"])],\n                preview={**dict(post), **cleaned, \"body_md\": values[\"body_md\"]})\n        content.update_post(conn, post, **values)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return editor_page(request, user, post, values, str(exc))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/edit\")\ndef back_to_editor(request: Request, post_id: int,\n                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Cancel on a Confirmation screen: the editor again, with the unsaved values.\"\"\"\n    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(load(conn, post_id)), **(values or {})})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef publish_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                 user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    try:\n        if values is not None:  # what's on the editor is what gets published\n            cleaned = clean(conn, post, values)\n        if not confirm:\n            shown = {**dict(post), **(cleaned if values is not None else {}),\n                     **({\"body_md\": values[\"body_md\"]} if values is not None else {})}\n            return confirmation(\n                request, user, post, action=\"publish_post\", button=\"Publish\",\n                heading=\"Confirm publishing\", pending=values, preview=shown,\n                summary=[f\"Publish “{shown['title']}”? It stays a Draft until you confirm, \"\n                         \"and reaches the public site at the next Export.\"])\n        if values is not None:\n            content.update_post(conn, post, **values)\n            post = load(conn, post_id)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return editor_page(request, user, post, values, str(exc))\n    content.publish_post(conn, post)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef unpublish_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, post, action=\"unpublish_post\", button=\"Unpublish\",\n            heading=\"Confirm unpublishing\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Unpublish “{post['title']}”? It returns to Draft and disappears \"\n                     \"from the public site at the next Export. Unsaved edits in the \"\n                     \"editor are not saved.\"])\n    content.unpublish_post(conn, post)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef delete_post(request: Request, post_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, post, action=\"delete_post\", button=\"Delete permanently\",\n            heading=\"Confirm deleting\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Delete “{post['title']}”? Deletion is permanent: the post \"\n                     \"can’t be recovered.\"]\n            + ([\"It leaves the public site at the next Export.\"]\n               if post[\"first_published_at\"] else []))\n    content.delete_post(conn, post)\n    return RedirectResponse(request.app.url_path_for(\"post_list\"), status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")\ndef preview(request: Request, post_id: int,\n            conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"public/post.html\",\n        {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", **article_context(dict(post))})\n\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n\nThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\nforget it. Pages and Users are Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.routes import posts\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n# Before the catch-all 404 at the bottom of this file.\nrouter.include_router(posts.router)\n\n\ndef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str, **ctx):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user, **ctx})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\",\n                not_live=content.changes_not_live(conn))\n\n\n@router.get(\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef pages(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Pages\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\ndef not_found(path: str):\n    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nTwo rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import content, db, settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.timeutil import fecha, fecha_evento, today_lima, utc_now\n\nFOLDERS = {\"news\": \"noticias\", \"event\": \"eventos\", \"menu\": \"menus\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\nHOME_NEWS = 5\nHOME_EVENTS = 3\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef _write(out: Path, name: str, html: str) -> None:\n    path = out / name\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(html)\n\n\ndef _event_label(post) -> str:\n    return fecha_evento(post[\"event_start_date\"], post[\"event_start_time\"],\n                        post[\"event_end_date\"])\n\n\ndef _date_label(post) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Events are dated by when they happen; News and Menus by first publish.\"\"\"\n    if post[\"kind\"] == \"event\":\n        return _event_label(post)\n    return fecha(post[\"first_published_at\"])\n\n\ndef _link(post, root: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"A feed entry. `root` is the relative path from the page back to site/.\"\"\"\n    return {\"title\": post[\"title\"],\n            \"href\": f\"{root}{FOLDERS[post['kind']]}/{post['slug']}.html\",\n            \"date\": _date_label(post)}\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None, export_date: date | None = None) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Write every Published Post into `out` and record the Export.\n\n    `export_date` (a Lima date) is what Upcoming is measured against from T04 on.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    export_date = export_date or today_lima()\n    env = environment()\n    db.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)  # an older database may lack `exports`\n    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        posts = content.published_posts(conn)\n        upcoming, past = content.published_events(conn, export_date)\n        current_menu, archive = content.published_menus(conn)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n\n    def page(template: str, depth: int, **ctx) -> str:\n        root = \"../\" * depth\n        nav = [(\"Inicio\", f\"{root}index.html\"),\n               (\"Noticias\", f\"{root}noticias/index.html\"),\n               (\"Eventos\", f\"{root}eventos/index.html\"),\n               (\"Menús anteriores\", f\"{root}menus/index.html\")]\n        return env.get_template(template).render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, lang=\"es\", nav=nav, css_path=f\"{root}style.css\",\n            home_path=f\"{root}index.html\", **ctx)\n\n    news = [p for p in posts if p[\"kind\"] == \"news\"]\n    menu = None\n    if current_menu:\n        menu = {**_link(current_menu, \"\"),\n                \"body_html\": render_markdown(current_menu[\"body_md\"])}\n    _write(out, \"index.html\", page(\n        \"public/home.html\", 0, menu=menu,\n        events=[_link(p, \"\") for p in upcoming[:HOME_EVENTS]],\n        news=[_link(p, \"\") for p in news[:HOME_NEWS]]))\n    _write(out, \"noticias/index.html\", page(\n        \"public/feed.html\", 1, page_title=\"Noticias\", heading=\"Noticias\",\n        items=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in news]))\n    _write(out, \"eventos/index.html\", page(\n        \"public/events.html\", 1, page_title=\"Eventos\",\n        upcoming=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in upcoming],\n        past=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in past]))\n    _write(out, \"menus/index.html\", page(\n        \"public/feed.html\", 1, page_title=\"Menús anteriores\",\n        heading=\"Menús anteriores\", items=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in archive]))\n    for p in posts:\n        event = p[\"kind\"] == \"event\"\n        _write(out, f\"{FOLDERS[p['kind']]}/{p['slug']}.html\", page(\n            \"public/post.html\", 1, page_title=p[\"title\"], post=p,\n            kind_label=KIND_LABELS_ES[p[\"kind\"]],\n            event_label=_event_label(p) if event else \"\",\n            date_label=\"\" if event else fecha(p[\"first_published_at\"]),\n            body_html=render_markdown(p[\"body_md\"])))\n\n    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO exports (ran_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    return out\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\nanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse, Response\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, publish, settings\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import console\nfrom app.web import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    db_path = Path(database_path) if database_path else settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(db_path)\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.db_path = db_path\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=8 * 60 * 60)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.LoginRequired)\n    def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.get(\"/style.css\")\n    def stylesheet():\n        # Lets the console's Preview load the public stylesheet.\n        return Response(publish.CSS, media_type=\"text/css\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"news\": []},\n        )\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    return app\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    kind              TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('news', 'event', 'menu')),\n    title             TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    body_md           TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                              CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT,\n    event_start_date  TEXT,\n    event_start_time  TEXT,\n    event_end_date    TEXT,\n    UNIQUE (kind, slug)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (\n    id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    ran_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\n-- One row per Post that had been Published and was then deleted. Counted as a\n-- change not yet Live until the next Export.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deleted_published (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    deleted_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\nEVENT_COLUMNS = (\"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\", \"event_end_date\")\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        # A database made before T04 has no Event date columns.\n        have = {row[\"name\"] for row in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(posts)\")}\n        for column in EVENT_COLUMNS:\n            if column not in have:\n                conn.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE posts ADD COLUMN {column} TEXT\")\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport jinja2\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.auth import csrf_token\nfrom app.timeutil import lima\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\ntemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\ntemplates.env.filters[\"lima\"] = lima\n\n\n@jinja2.pass_context\ndef path_for(context, name: str, **params) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The path of a named console route, e.g. path_for('edit_post', post_id=3).\n\n    Unlike Starlette's url_for it stays a path (\"/admin/posts/3\"), not a full URL.\n    \"\"\"\n    return context[\"request\"].app.url_path_for(name, **params)\n\n\ntemplates.env.globals[\"path_for\"] = path_for\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\n","numLines":868,"startLine":1,"totalLines":868}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2ab4f261-4b4a-4d29-8b0a-b6b61957fd5a","session_id":"ce0946df-6232-4faa-8a60-041a32ad4432","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6849c5d9-d2dc-4a6e-802c-433f2aa1aa8f","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}
