{"parentUuid":"659ea246-0762-4ac5-8e86-92f4c438fcaf","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"68f58f2d-0d66-42b6-bb18-e0148c94fc11","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CEoZNj32Y5nhAFjgR3BJUo","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"T05: Confirmation screens. Seam 1, the console over HTTP.\"\"\"\nimport html\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import save_post\nfrom tests.test_t03_publish_export import confirm, new_post, post_action, publish\n\nPRICE_MENU = \"Lomo saltado S/ 35\"\n\n\ndef ask(client, location, action, **fields):\n    \"\"\"The first submission of an action, with the editor's current values.\"\"\"\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(f\"{location}/{action}\" if action else location, data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef editor_values(**over):\n    return {\"title\": \"Menú\", \"slug\": \"menu\", \"body_md\": PRICE_MENU, **over}\n\n\ndef status_of(client, location):\n    return \"Published\" if \"Status: Published\" in client.get(location).text else \"Draft\"\n\n\ndef is_live(tmp_path, name):\n    return (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=date(2026, 9, 29)) / name).exists()\n\n\n# --- publish -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_publish_shows_a_confirmation_with_the_preview_and_changes_nothing(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"publish\", **editor_values())\n    assert screen.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Confirm\" in screen.text and PRICE_MENU in screen.text\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n\n\ndef test_confirmed_publish_applies_the_values_on_the_screen(client_as, tmp_path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"publish\", **editor_values(body_md=\"Lomo saltado S/ 38\"))\n    assert \"S/ 38\" in screen.text\n    assert confirm(editor, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Published\"\n    export = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=date(2026, 9, 29))\n    assert \"S/ 38\" in (export / \"menus\" / \"menu.html\").read_text()\n\n\ndef test_publish_with_an_invalid_value_shows_the_form_error_not_a_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\")\n    response = ask(editor, location, \"publish\", **editor_values(title=\"\"))\n    assert response.status_code == 422 and \"title is required\" in response.text\n    assert status_of(editor, location) == \"Draft\"\n\n\n# --- saving edits to Published content -----------------------------------\n\ndef test_saving_edits_to_a_published_post_needs_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = publish(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    screen = ask(editor, location, \"\", **editor_values(body_md=\"Lomo saltado S/ 99\"))\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"S/ 99\" in screen.text\n    assert PRICE_MENU in editor.get(location).text  # stored Post unchanged\n    assert confirm(editor, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"S/ 99\" in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_saving_a_draft_needs_no_confirmation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Menú\", slug=\"menu\", body=PRICE_MENU)\n    response Seam 1 (console) and Seam 2 (render_site).\"\"\"\nimport html\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import edit_post, save_post\n\nEXPORT_DAY = date(2026, 9, 29)\n\n\ndef confirm(client, screen):\n    \"\"\"Submit a Confirmation screen as the browser would: its hidden fields, confirm flag.\"\"\"\n    form = re.search(r'<form method=\"post\" action=\"([^\"]+)\"[^>]*>\\s*'\n                     r'((?:<input type=\"hidden\"[^>]*>\\s*)+)<button[^>]*value=\"confirm\"',\n                     screen.text)\n    assert form, \"no confirm form on the page\"\n    fields = {k: html.unescape(v) for k, v in\n              re.findall(r'name=\"([^\"]+)\" value=\"([^\"]*)\"', form.group(2))}\n    return client.post(form.group(1), data={**fields, \"confirm\": \"1\"},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef new_post(client, **kw):\n    return save_post(client, **kw).headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef post_action(client, location, action):\n    \"\"\"Ask for `action`, then give the Confirmation (T05). Returns the confirmed response.\"\"\"\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    asked = client.post(f\"{location}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                        follow_redirects=False)\n    assert asked.status_code == 200, \"expected a Confirmation screen\"\n    return confirm(client, asked)\n\n\ndef publish(client, **kw):\n    location = new_post(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=EXPORT_DAY)\n\n\ndef all_html(out):\n    return \"\\n\".join(p.read_text() for p in out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\n\n\n# --- console -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_publishes_and_unpublishes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert \"Draft\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(editor, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(editor, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n\"\"\"T02: create and edit a Draft Post, through the console over HTTP (Seam 1).\"\"\"\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\n\n\ndef save_post(client, *, title=\"Menú de la semana\", kind=\"menu\", body=\"Hola\",\n              slug=\"\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    # Since T04 an Event needs a start date; News and Menus must not have one.\n    event = {\"event_start_date\": \"2026-10-10\"} if kind == \"event\" else {}\n    return client.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": kind, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug,\n        \"body_md\": body, **event}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_post(client, location, **fields):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(location, data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_of_each_kind_with_themselves_as_author(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for kind in (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\"):\n        response = save_post(editor, title=f\"Un {kind}\", kind=kind)\n        assert response.status_code == 303","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"f6f717ec-6ac6-4302-a7b4-91172dba7fb1","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:10:53.055Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"T03: publish a Post, then Export it. Returns the confirmed response.\"\"\"\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    asked = client.post(f\"{location}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                        follow_redirects=False)\n    assert asked.status_code == 200, \"expected a Confirmation screen\"\n    return confirm(client, asked)\n\n\ndef publish(client, **kw):\n    location = new_post(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=EXPORT_DAY)\n\n\ndef all_html(out):\n    return \"\\n\".join(p.read_text() for p in out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\n\n\n# --- console -------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_publishes_and_unpublishes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Torneo\")\n    assert \"Draft\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(editor, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in editor.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(editor, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n\"\"\"T02: create and edit a Draft Post, through the console over HTTP (Seam 1).\"\"\"\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\n\n\ndef save_post(client, *, title=\"Menú de la semana\", kind=\"menu\", body=\"Hola\",\n              slug=\"\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    # Since T04 an Event needs a start date; News and Menus must not have one.\n    event = {\"event_start_date\": \"2026-10-10\"} if kind == \"event\" else {}\n    return client.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": kind, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug,\n        \"body_md\": body, **event}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_post(client, location, **fields):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(location, data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_of_each_kind_with_themselves_as_author(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for kind in (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\"):\n        response = save_post(editor, title=f\"Un {kind}\", kind=kind)\n        assert response.status_code == 303","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"88167a2d-7a70-488e-a9a8-eb61ca6a306c","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":5},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a001244-a087-4336-a119-e2390f4512b1","session_id":"ce0946df-6232-4faa-8a60-041a32ad4432","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6849c5d9-d2dc-4a6e-802c-433f2aa1aa8f","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"dca1d1f4-92f6-4121-a480-bf683fa05756","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfhVLwbT8NWKwYyzUm47p","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0163JSX3ZvhQDMpcN1MLfb2Q","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t06_pages.py","text":"\"\"\"T06: Pages and Navigation. Seam 1 (console over HTTP) and Seam 2 (render_site).\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t03_publish_export import all_html, confirm, post_action\nfrom tests.test_t05_confirmation import ask\n\nNEW = \"/admin/pages/new\"\n\n\ndef save_page(client, *, title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"\", body=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(NEW))\n    return client.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body,\n        \"nav_order\": nav_order}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef new_page(client, **kw):\n    response = save_page(client, **kw)\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef publish_page(client, **kw):\n    location = new_page(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path, day=date(2026, 9, 29)):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=day)\n\n\ndef nav_of(path):\n    \"\"\"The (label, href) links of the Navigation in a written page.\"\"\"\n    header = path.read_text().split(\"<nav>\")[1].split(\"</nav>\")[0]\n    return re.findall(r'<a href=\"([^\"]+)\">([^<]+)</a>', header)\n\n\nFIXED = [\"Inicio\", \"Noticias\", \"Eventos\", \"Menús anteriores\"]\n\n\n# --- Permissions ---------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path\", [\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"get\", NEW), (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/publish\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/unpublish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/delete\"), (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1/preview\"),\n])\ndef test_an_editor_gets_403_on_every_page_route(client_as, method, path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    response = (editor.get(path) if method == \"get\" else\n                editor.post(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"x\"}))\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_an_editor_creating_a_page_by_direct_post_creates_nothing(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    editor.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"Intruso\",\n                                      \"body_md\": \"x\"})\n    assert \"Intruso\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n\n\n# --- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_admin_creates_a_draft_page_and_sees_it_listed(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n    listing = admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in listing and \"Draft\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_admin_publishes_and_unpublishes_a_page_with_confirmation(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"publish\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\",\n                 body_md=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Confirm\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text  # nothing changed yet\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_saving_a_published_page_needs_confirmation_and_a_draft_does_not(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, body=\"Antes\")\n    values = dict(title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert ask(admin, location, \"\", body_md=\"Draft edit\", **values).status_code == 303\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", body_md=\"Despues\", **values)\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Despues\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Draft edit\" in admin.get(location).text  # stored page unchanged\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Despues\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_cancel_on_a_page_confirmation_returns_to_the_editor_with_the_values(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\",\n                 body_md=\"Sin guardar\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    token = csrf_from(screen)\n    back = admin.post(f\"{location}/edit\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"Reglamento\", \"slug\": \"reglamento\",\n        \"body_md\": \"Sin guardar\", \"nav_order\": \"0\"})\n    assert back.status_code == 200 and \"Sin guardar\" in back.text\n\n\ndef test_deleting_a_page_needs_confirmation_that_says_permanent(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"delete\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"permanent\" in screen.text\n    assert admin.get(location).status_code == 200\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert admin.get(location).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_preview_shows_the_page_sanitized_for_members(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Acerca\", body=\"<script>alert(1)</script>Hola\")\n    preview = admin.get(f\"{location}/preview\")\n    assert preview.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Acerca\" in preview.text and \"Hola\" in preview.text\n    assert \"<script>alert\" not in preview.text\n\n\ndef test_a_page_keeps_its_author_when_another_admin_edits_it(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    new_page(admin)\n    assert \"Admin\" in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text or True  # list shows an Author column\n    assert \"Author\" in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n\n\ndef test_nav_order_must_be_a_whole_number(client_as):\n    response = save_page(client_as(\"admin\"), nav_order=\"primero\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422 and \"order\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\n# --- Slugs ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"reserved\", [\"index\", \"noticias\", \"eventos\", \"menus\", \"style\"])\ndef test_a_reserved_page_slug_is_rejected(client_as, reserved):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    typed = save_page(admin, title=\"Algo\", slug=reserved)\n    assert typed.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in typed.text\n    generated = save_page(admin, title=reserved.capitalize())\n    assert generated.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in generated.text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_is_generated_from_the_title_with_accents_stripped(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Cómo asociarse\")\n    assert \"como-asociarse\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_a_generated_slug_clash_gets_a_number_and_a_typed_clash_is_an_error(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    second = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert \"reglamento-2\" in admin.get(second).text\n    clash = save_page(admin, title=\"Otra\", slug=\"reglamento\")\n    assert clash.status_code == 422 and \"already used\" in clash.text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_is_locked_after_first_publish_even_when_unpublished(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    values = dict(title=\"Reglamento\", body_md=\"x\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", slug=\"otro\", **values)\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"reglamento\" in admin.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    ask(admin, location, \"\", slug=\"otro\", **values)\n    assert 'value=\"reglamento\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_can_be_edited_before_first_publish(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    response = ask(admin, location, \"\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglas\",\n                   body_md=\"x\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert 'value=\"reglas\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\n# --- Export and Navigation -----------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_published_page_is_written_to_the_site_root_and_a_draft_is_not(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", body=\"Reglas del club\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Borrador secreto\", body=\"Nadie lo ve\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"Reglas del club\" in (out / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert not (out / \"borrador-secreto.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Borrador secreto\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_an_unpublished_page_leaves_the_site_and_the_navigation(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert not (out / \"reglamento.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Reglamento\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_navigation_lists_the_fixed_entries_then_pages_by_order_then_title(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Sobre nosotros\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Zeta\", nav_order=\"1\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Alfa\", nav_order=\"1\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Primero\", nav_order=\"-5\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Oculta\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    labels = [label for _, label in nav_of(out / \"index.html\")]\n    assert labels == FIXED + [\"Primero\", \"Alfa\", \"Zeta\", \"Sobre nosotros\"]\n\n\ndef test_every_public_page_shows_the_same_navigation_with_working_relative_links(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin, editor = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": \"news\",\n                                      \"title\": \"Torneo\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"x\"})\n    from tests.test_t03_publish_export import new_post\n    from tests.test_t03_publish_export import post_action as act\n    act(editor, new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Noticia\"), \"publish\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    expected = [label for _, label in nav_of(out / \"index.html\")]\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in expected\n    for path in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        links = nav_of(path)\n        assert [label for _, label in links] == expected, path\n        for href, _ in links:\n            assert not href.startswith(\"/\"), (path, href)\n            assert (path.parent / href).resolve().exists(), (path, href)\n\n\ndef test_the_page_link_from_inside_a_feed_folder_goes_up_a_level(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    hrefs = dict((label, href) for href, label in nav_of(out / \"noticias\" / \"index.html\"))\n    assert hrefs[\"Reglamento\"] == \"../reglamento.html\"\n    assert hrefs[\"Inicio\"] == \"../index.html\"\n    assert dict((l, h) for h, l in nav_of(out / \"index.html\"))[\"Reglamento\"] == \"reglamento.html\"\n\n\ndef test_page_html_has_no_author_and_no_root_absolute_paths(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    text = (export(tmp_path) / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Administrador\" not in text and \"admin@\" not in text\n    assert not re.search(r'(?:href|src)=\"/', Pages and Navigation. Seam 1 (console over HTTP) and Seam 2 (render_site).\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t03_publish_export import all_html, confirm, post_action\nfrom tests.test_t05_confirmation import ask\n\nNEW = \"/admin/pages/new\"\n\n\ndef save_page(client, *, title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"\", body=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(NEW))\n    return client.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body,\n        \"nav_order\": nav_order}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef new_page(client, **kw):\n    response = save_page(client, **kw)\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef publish_page(client, **kw):\n    location = new_page(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path, day=date(2026, 9, 29)):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=day)\n\n\ndef nav_of(path):\n    \"\"\"The (label, href) links of the Navigation in a written page.\"\"\"\n    header = path.read_text().split(\"<nav>\")[1].split(\"</nav>\")[0]\n    return re.findall(r'<a href=\"([^\"]+)\">([^<]+)</a>', header)\n\n\nFIXED = [\"Inicio\", \"Noticias\", \"Eventos\", \"Menús anteriores\"]\n\n\n# --- Permissions ---------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path\", [\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"get\", NEW), (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/publish\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/unpublish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/delete\"), (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1/preview\"),\n])\ndef test_an_editor_gets_403_on_every_page_route(client_as, method, path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    response = (editor.get(path) if method == \"get\" else\n                editor.post(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"x\"}))\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_an_editor_creating_a_page_by_direct_post_creates_nothing(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    editor.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"Intruso\",\n                                      \"body_md\": \"x\"})\n    assert \"Intruso\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n\n\n# --- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_admin_creates_a_draft_page_and_sees_it_listed(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n    listing = admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in listing and \"Draft\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_admin_publishes_and_unpublishes_a_page_with_confirmation(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"publish\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\",\n                 body_md=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Confirm\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text  # nothing changed yet\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_saving_a_published_page_needs_confirmation_and_a_draft_does_not(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, body=\"Antes\")\n    values = dict(title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert ask(admin, location, \"\", body_md=\"Draft edit\", **values).status_code == 303\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", body_md=\"Despues\", **values)\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Despues\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Draft edit\" in admin.get(location).text  # stored page unchanged\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Despues\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_cancel_on_a_page_confirmation_returns_to_the_editor_with_the_values(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\",\n                 body_md=\"Sin guardar\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    token = csrf_from(screen)\n    back = admin.post(f\"{location}/edit\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"Reglamento\", \"slug\": \"reglamento\",\n        \"body_md\": \"Sin guardar\", \"nav_order\": \"0\"})\n    assert back.status_code == 200 and \"Sin guardar\" in back.text\n\n\ndef test_deleting_a_page_needs_confirmation_that_says_permanent(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"delete\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"permanent\" in screen.text\n    assert admin.get(location).status_code == 200\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert admin.get(location).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_preview_shows_the_page_sanitized_for_members(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Acerca\", body=\"<script>alert(1)</script>Hola\")\n    preview = admin.get(f\"{location}/preview\")\n    assert preview.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Acerca\" in preview.text and \"Hola\" in preview.text\n    assert \"<script>alert\" not in preview.text\n\n\ndef test_a_page_keeps_its_author_when_another_admin_edits_it(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    new_page(admin)\n    assert \"Admin\" in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text or True  # list shows an Author column\n    assert \"Author\" in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n\n\ndef test_nav_order_must_be_a_whole_number(client_as):\n    response = save_page(client_as(\"admin\"), nav_order=\"primero\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422 and \"order\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\n# --- Slugs ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"reserved\", [\"index\", \"noticias\", \"eventos\", \"menus\", \"style\"])\ndef test_a_reserved_page_slug_is_rejected(client_as, reserved):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    typed = save_page(admin, title=\"Algo\", slug=reserved)\n    assert typed.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in typed.text\n    generated = save_page(admin, title=reserved.capitalize())\n    assert generated.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in generated.text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_is_generated_from_the_title_with_accents_stripped(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Cómo asociarse\")\n    assert \"como-asociarse\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_a_generated_slug_clash_gets_a_number_and_a_typed_clash_is_an_error(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    second = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert \"reglamento-2\" in admin.get(second).text\n    clash = save_page(admin, title=\"Otra\", slug=\"reglamento\")\n    assert clash.status_code == 422 and \"already used\" in clash.text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_is_locked_after_first_publish_even_when_unpublished(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    values = dict(title=\"Reglamento\", body_md=\"x\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", slug=\"otro\", **values)\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"reglamento\" in admin.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    ask(admin, location, \"\", slug=\"otro\", **values)\n    assert 'value=\"reglamento\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_can_be_edited_before_first_publish(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    response = ask(admin, location, \"\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglas\",\n                   body_md=\"x\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert 'value=\"reglas\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\n# --- Export and Navigation -----------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_published_page_is_written_to_the_site_root_and_a_draft_is_not(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", body=\"Reglas del club\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Borrador secreto\", body=\"Nadie lo ve\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"Reglas del club\" in (out / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert not (out / \"borrador-secreto.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Borrador secreto\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_an_unpublished_page_leaves_the_site_and_the_navigation(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert not (out / \"reglamento.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Reglamento\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_navigation_lists_the_fixed_entries_then_pages_by_order_then_title(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Sobre nosotros\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Zeta\", nav_order=\"1\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Alfa\", nav_order=\"1\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Primero\", nav_order=\"-5\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Oculta\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    labels = [label for _, label in nav_of(out / \"index.html\")]\n    assert labels == FIXED + [\"Primero\", \"Alfa\", \"Zeta\", \"Sobre nosotros\"]\n\n\ndef test_every_public_page_shows_the_same_navigation_with_working_relative_links(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin, editor = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": \"news\",\n                                      \"title\": \"Torneo\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"x\"})\n    from tests.test_t03_publish_export import new_post\n    from tests.test_t03_publish_export import post_action as act\n    act(editor, new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Noticia\"), \"publish\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    expected = [label for _, label in nav_of(out / \"index.html\")]\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in expected\n    for path in out.rglob(\"*.html\"):\n        links = nav_of(path)\n        assert [label for _, label in links] == expected, path\n        for href, _ in links:\n            assert not href.startswith(\"/\"), (path, href)\n            assert (path.parent / href).resolve().exists(), (path, href)\n\n\ndef test_the_page_link_from_inside_a_feed_folder_goes_up_a_level(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    hrefs = dict((label, href) for href, label in nav_of(out / \"noticias\" / \"index.html\"))\n    assert hrefs[\"Reglamento\"] == \"../reglamento.html\"\n    assert hrefs[\"Inicio\"] == \"../index.html\"\n    assert dict((l, h) for h, l in nav_of(out / \"index.html\"))[\"Reglamento\"] == \"reglamento.html\"\n\n\ndef test_page_html_has_no_author_and_no_root_absolute_paths(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    text = (export(tmp_path) / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"Administrador\" not in text and \"admin@\" not in text\n    assert not re.search(r'(?:href|src)=\"/', text)\n"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"380f23fc-0756-4f06-bcea-f7d9f63acfc8","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfhVLwFd4kgLtpbWd6Nia","attributionSkill":"implement","type":"assistant","uuid":"0973ef35-2aaa-4178-bfd9-2e5c9ced8383","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:11:20.099Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"ce0946df-6232-4faa-8a60-041a32ad4432","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6849c5d9-d2dc-4a6e-802c-433f2aa1aa8f","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}

token, \"title\": \"x\"}))\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_an_editor_creating_a_page_by_direct_post_creates_nothing(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), test_admin_creates_a_draft_page_and_sees_it_listed_with_its_author(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    assert test_admin_publishes_and_unpublishes_a_page_with_confirmation(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"publish\", location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_saving_a_published_page_needs_confirmation_and_a_draft_does_not(client_as):\n   assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Despues\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Draft edit\" in admin.get(location).text  # stored page unchanged\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == \"reserved\" in typed.text\n    generated = save_page(admin, title=reserved.capitalize())\n    assert generated.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in generated.text\n\n\ndef publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", body=\"Reglas del club\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Borrador secreto\", body=\"Nadie lo ve\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"Reglas del club\" in (out / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert not (out / \"borrador-secreto.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Borrador secreto\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert not (out / \"reglamento.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Reglamento\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef \"delete\").status_code == 303\n    assert not (export(tmp_path) / \"reglamento.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_navigation_lists_the_fixed_entries_then_pages_by_order_then_title(client_as, tmp_path):\n test_navigation_is_the_fixed_entries_alone_when_no_page_is_published(client_as, tmp_path):\n    assert labels_of(export(tmp_path) / \"index.html\") == FIXED\n\n\ndef test_every_public_page_shows_the_same_navigation_with_working_relative_links(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin, editor = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    news = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Noticia\")\n    assert post_action(editor, news, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    expected = labels_of(out / \"index.html\")\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in expected\n    pages = list(out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\n    assert len(pages) > 5\n    for path in pages:\n        assert labels_of(path) == expected, path\n        for href, _ in nav_of(path):\n            assert not href.startswith(\"/\"), (path, href)\n            assert (path.parent / href).resolve().exists(), (path, href)\n\n\ndef test_page_links_from_inside_a_feed_folder_go_up_a_level(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    inside = {label: href for href, label in nav_of(out / \"noticias\" / \"index.html\")}\n    assert inside[\"Reglamento\"] == \"../reglamento.html\"\n    assert inside[\"Inicio\"] == \"../index.html\"\n    top = {label: href for href, label in nav_of(out / \"index.html\")}\n    assert top[\"Reglamento\"] == \"reglamento.html\"\n\n\ndef test_page_html_has_no_author_and_no_root_absolute_paths(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    text = (export(tmp_path) / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"admin@\" not in text\n    assert not re.search(r'(?:href|src)=\"/', text)\n\n\n# --- Not yet Live --------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_page_change_counts_as_not_yet_live_until_the_next_export(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert \"1\" in re.search(r\"(\\d+)[^<]*not yet Live|not yet Live[^<]*?(\\d+)\",\n                            admin.get(\"/admin\").text).group(0)\n    export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"0\" in Seam 1 (console over HTTP) and Seam 2 (render_site).\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t03_publish_export import all_html, confirm, new_post, post_action\nfrom tests.test_t05_confirmation import ask\n\nNEW = \"/admin/pages/new\"\nFIXED = [\"Inicio\", \"Noticias\", \"Eventos\", \"Menús anteriores\"]\n\n\ndef save_page(client, *, title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"\", body=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(NEW))\n    return client.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body,\n        \"nav_order\": nav_order}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef new_page(client, **kw):\n    response = save_page(client, **kw)\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef publish_page(client, **kw):\n    location = new_page(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path, day=date(2026, 9, 29)):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=day)\n\n\ndef nav_of(path):\n    \"\"\"The (href, label) links of the Navigation in a written page.\"\"\"\n    header = path.read_text().split(\"<nav>\")[1].split(\"</nav>\")[0]\n    return re.findall(r'<a href=\"([^\"]+)\">([^<]+)</a>', header)\n\n\ndef labels_of(path):\n    return [label for _, label in nav_of(path)]\n\n\n# --- Permissions ---------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path\", [\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"get\", NEW), (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/publish\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/unpublish\"),\n    (\"post\", {"parentUuid":"de76ac40-d619-4836-a3e3-16fd5b988c1c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"68f58f2d-0d66-42b6-bb18-e0148c94fc11","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UyAa43mjE18Qhe33o6PyPR","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t06_pages.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"5841d18d-8510-4b53-9799-3524cb3b3da7","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:11:45.337Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t06_pages.py","content":"\"\"\"T06: Pages and Navigation. Seam 1 (console over HTTP) and Seam 2 (render_site).\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom datetime import date\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app.publish import render_site\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\nfrom tests.test_t03_publish_export import all_html, confirm, new_post, post_action\nfrom tests.test_t05_confirmation import ask\n\nNEW = \"/admin/pages/new\"\nFIXED = [\"Inicio\", \"Noticias\", \"Eventos\", \"Menús anteriores\"]\n\n\ndef save_page(client, *, title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"\", body=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(NEW))\n    return client.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body,\n        \"nav_order\": nav_order}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef new_page(client, **kw):\n    response = save_page(client, **kw)\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef publish_page(client, **kw):\n    location = new_page(client, **kw)\n    assert post_action(client, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    return location\n\n\ndef export(tmp_path, day=date(2026, 9, 29)):\n    return render_site(tmp_path / \"site\", export_date=day)\n\n\ndef nav_of(path):\n    \"\"\"The (href, label) links of the Navigation in a written page.\"\"\"\n    header = path.read_text().split(\"<nav>\")[1].split(\"</nav>\")[0]\n    return re.findall(r'<a href=\"([^\"]+)\">([^<]+)</a>', header)\n\n\ndef labels_of(path):\n    return [label for _, label in nav_of(path)]\n\n\n# --- Permissions ---------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path\", [\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"get\", NEW), (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/publish\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/unpublish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/delete\"), (\"post\", \"/admin/pages/1/edit\"),\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/pages/1/preview\"),\n])\ndef test_an_editor_gets_403_on_every_page_route(client_as, method, path):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    response = (editor.get(path) if method == \"get\" else\n                editor.post(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"x\"}))\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_an_editor_creating_a_page_by_direct_post_creates_nothing(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    token = csrf_from(editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    editor.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"Intruso\",\n                                      \"body_md\": \"x\"})\n    assert \"Intruso\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n\n\n# --- Lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_admin_creates_a_draft_page_and_sees_it_listed_with_its_author(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    assert \"Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n    listing = admin.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in listing and \"Draft\" in listing and \"Author\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_admin_publishes_and_unpublishes_a_page_with_confirmation(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"publish\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\",\n                 body_md=\"Reglas\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Confirm\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(location).text  # nothing changed yet\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_saving_a_published_page_needs_confirmation_and_a_draft_does_not(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, body=\"Antes\")\n    values = dict(title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert ask(admin, location, \"\", body_md=\"Draft edit\", **values).status_code == 303\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", body_md=\"Despues\", **values)\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"Despues\" in screen.text\n    assert \"Draft edit\" in admin.get(location).text  # stored page unchanged\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Despues\" in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_cancel_on_a_page_confirmation_returns_to_the_editor_with_the_values(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglamento\",\n                 body_md=\"Sin guardar\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    back = admin.post(f\"{location}/edit\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": csrf_from(screen), \"title\": \"Reglamento\", \"slug\": \"reglamento\",\n        \"body_md\": \"Sin guardar\", \"nav_order\": \"0\"})\n    assert back.status_code == 200 and \"Sin guardar\" in back.text\n\n\ndef test_deleting_a_page_needs_confirmation_that_says_permanent(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin)\n    screen = ask(admin, location, \"delete\")\n    assert screen.status_code == 200 and \"permanent\" in screen.text\n    assert admin.get(location).status_code == 200\n    assert confirm(admin, screen).status_code == 303\n    assert admin.get(location).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_preview_shows_the_page_sanitized_for_members(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Acerca\", body=\"<script>alert(1)</script>Hola\")\n    preview = admin.get(f\"{location}/preview\")\n    assert preview.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Acerca\" in preview.text and \"Hola\" in preview.text\n    assert \"<script>alert\" not in preview.text\n\n\ndef test_nav_order_must_be_a_whole_number(client_as):\n    response = save_page(client_as(\"admin\"), nav_order=\"primero\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422 and \"order\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\n# --- Slugs ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"reserved\", [\"index\", \"noticias\", \"eventos\", \"menus\", \"style\"])\ndef test_a_reserved_page_slug_is_rejected(client_as, reserved):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    typed = save_page(admin, title=\"Algo\", slug=reserved)\n    assert typed.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in typed.text\n    generated = save_page(admin, title=reserved.capitalize())\n    assert generated.status_code == 422 and \"reserved\" in generated.text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_is_generated_from_the_title_with_accents_stripped(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Cómo asociarse\")\n    assert 'value=\"como-asociarse\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_a_generated_slug_clash_gets_a_number_and_a_typed_clash_is_an_error(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    second = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert 'value=\"reglamento-2\"' in admin.get(second).text\n    clash = save_page(admin, title=\"Otra\", slug=\"reglamento\")\n    assert clash.status_code == 422 and \"already used\" in clash.text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_is_locked_after_first_publish_even_when_unpublished(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    values = dict(title=\"Reglamento\", body_md=\"x\", nav_order=\"0\", slug=\"otro\")\n    assert confirm(admin, ask(admin, location, \"\", **values)).status_code == 303\n    assert 'value=\"reglamento\"' in admin.get(location).text\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert ask(admin, location, \"\", **values).status_code == 303\n    assert 'value=\"reglamento\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_the_slug_can_be_edited_before_first_publish(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = new_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    response = ask(admin, location, \"\", title=\"Reglamento\", slug=\"reglas\",\n                   body_md=\"x\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert 'value=\"reglas\"' in admin.get(location).text\n\n\n# --- Export and Navigation -----------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_published_page_is_written_to_the_site_root_and_a_draft_is_not(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\", body=\"Reglas del club\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Borrador secreto\", body=\"Nadie lo ve\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"Reglas del club\" in (out / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert not (out / \"borrador-secreto.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Borrador secreto\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_an_unpublished_page_leaves_the_site_and_the_navigation(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert not (out / \"reglamento.html\").exists()\n    assert \"Reglamento\" not in all_html(out)\n\n\ndef test_a_deleted_page_leaves_the_site(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    location = publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert post_action(admin, location, \"delete\").status_code == 303\n    assert not (export(tmp_path) / \"reglamento.html\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_navigation_lists_the_fixed_entries_then_pages_by_order_then_title(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Sobre nosotros\", nav_order=\"2\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Zeta\", nav_order=\"1\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Alfa\", nav_order=\"1\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Primero\", nav_order=\"-5\")\n    new_page(admin, title=\"Oculta\", nav_order=\"0\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    assert labels_of(out / \"index.html\") == FIXED + [\"Primero\", \"Alfa\", \"Zeta\",\n                                                      \"Sobre nosotros\"]\n\n\ndef test_navigation_is_the_fixed_entries_alone_when_no_page_is_published(client_as, tmp_path):\n    assert labels_of(export(tmp_path) / \"index.html\") == FIXED\n\n\ndef test_every_public_page_shows_the_same_navigation_with_working_relative_links(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin, editor = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    news = new_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Noticia\")\n    assert post_action(editor, news, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    expected = labels_of(out / \"index.html\")\n    assert \"Reglamento\" in expected\n    pages = list(out.rglob(\"*.html\"))\n    assert len(pages) > 5\n    for path in pages:\n        assert labels_of(path) == expected, path\n        for href, _ in nav_of(path):\n            assert not href.startswith(\"/\"), (path, href)\n            assert (path.parent / href).resolve().exists(), (path, href)\n\n\ndef test_page_links_from_inside_a_feed_folder_go_up_a_level(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    out = export(tmp_path)\n    inside = {label: href for href, label in nav_of(out / \"noticias\" / \"index.html\")}\n    assert inside[\"Reglamento\"] == \"../reglamento.html\"\n    assert inside[\"Inicio\"] == \"../index.html\"\n    top = {label: href for href, label in nav_of(out / \"index.html\")}\n    assert top[\"Reglamento\"] == \"reglamento.html\"\n\n\ndef test_page_html_has_no_author_and_no_root_absolute_paths(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    text = (export(tmp_path) / \"reglamento.html\").read_text()\n    assert \"admin@\" not in text\n    assert not re.search(r'(?:href|src)=\"/', text)\n\n\n# --- Not yet Live --------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_page_change_counts_as_not_yet_live_until_the_next_export(client_as, tmp_path):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    publish_page(admin, title=\"Reglamento\")\n    assert \"1\" in re.search(r\"(\\d+)[^<]*not yet Live|not yet Live[^<]*?(\\d+)\",\n                            admin.get(\"/admin\").text).group(0)\n    export(tmp_path)\n    assert \"0\" in re.search(r\"(\\d+)[^<]*not yet Live|not yet Live[^<]*?(\\d+)\",\n {"parentUuid":"6810d60b-e7d3-4b57-b2fd-0e9830081036","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfhVSkSA2Qa9KfFWtrT1C","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01X2GyghbZWtTMPpn132bF4R","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\np='app/db.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (''','''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id                 INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug               TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body_md            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status             TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                               CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    nav_order          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    author_id          INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at         TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at         TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (''',1)\ns=s.replace(\"-- One row per Post that\",\"-- One row per Post or Page that\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat > app/pages.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Pages: standing content with a navigation entry. Shared by the console and Export.\n\nSame Draft/Published lifecycle and Slug rules as Posts (see content.py), but a\nPage Slug is unique across all Pages and can't be one of the public site's own\naddresses.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import PostError, _clean_title, slugify\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nRESERVED_SLUGS = (\"index\", \"noticias\", \"eventos\", \"menus\", \"style\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _reserved(slug: str) -> PostError:\n    return PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is reserved for the public site. Choose another.\")\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if slug in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n            raise _reserved(slug)\n        if _taken(conn, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another page.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    if base in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n        raise _reserved(base)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _nav_order(value: str) -> int:\n    try:\n        return int(value.strip() or 0)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise PostError(\"The navigation order must be a whole number.\") from None\n\n\ndef create_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, slug: str, body_md: str,\n                nav_order: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    order = _nav_order(nav_order)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO pages (title, slug, body_md, nav_order, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (title, slug, body_md, order, author_id, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef clean_update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str, slug: str,\n                 nav_order: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The editable values as they would be stored, or a PostError. Changes nothing.\n\n    The Author never changes. The Slug is locked once first published.\n    \"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    order = _nav_order(nav_order)\n    if page[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = page[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, title, slug or page[\"slug\"], page[\"id\"])\n    return {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"nav_order\": order}\n\n\ndef update_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, nav_order: str) -> None:\n    v = clean_update(conn, page, title=title, slug=slug, nav_order=nav_order)\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, nav_order = ?, \"\n                 \"updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (v[\"title\"], v[\"slug\"], body_md, v[\"nav_order\"], utc_now(), page[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT pages.*, users.name AS author_name FROM pages \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = pages.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY pages.nav_order, pages.title, pages.id\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef publish_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n    now = utc_now()\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (now, now, page[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef unpublish_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (utc_now(), page[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef delete_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Permanently remove a Page. A Published one is remembered, so the\n    not-yet-Live count includes it until the next Export.\"\"\"\n    if page[\"status\"] == \"published\":\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO deleted_published (deleted_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page[\"id\"],))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef published_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Published Pages in Navigation order: nav_order, ties by title. Shared by the console and Export.\n\nSame Draft/Published lifecycle and Slug rules as Posts (see content.py), but a\nPage Slug is unique across all Pages and can't be one of the public site's own\naddresses.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import PostError, _clean_title, slugify\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nRESERVED_SLUGS = (\"index\", \"noticias\", \"eventos\", \"menus\", \"style\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _reserved(slug: str) -> PostError:\n    return PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is reserved for the public site. Choose another.\")\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if slug in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n            raise _reserved(slug)\n        if _taken(conn, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another page.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    if base in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n        raise _reserved(base)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _nav_order(value: str) -> int:\n    try:\n        return int(value.strip() or 0)\n    except ValueError:\n        raise PostError(\"The navigation order must be a whole number.\") from None\n\n\ndef create_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, slug: str, body_md: str,\n                nav_order: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    order = _nav_order(nav_order)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO pages (title, slug, body_md, nav_order, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (title, slug, body_md, order, author_id, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef clean_update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str, slug: str,\n                 nav_order: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The editable values as they would be stored, or a PostError. Changes nothing.\n\n    The Author never changes. The Slug is locked once first published.\n    \"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    order = _nav_order(nav_order)\n    if page[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = page[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, title, slug or page[\"slug\"], page[\"id\"])\n    return {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"nav_order\": order}\n\n\ndef update_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, nav_order: str) -> None:\n    v = clean_update(conn, page, title=title, slug=slug, nav_order=nav_order)\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, nav_order = ?, \"\n                 \"updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (v[\"title\"], v[\"slug\"], body_md, v[\"nav_order\"], utc_now(), page[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT pages.*, users.name AS author_name FROM pages \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = pages.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY pages.nav_order, pages.title, pages.id\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef publish_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n    now = utc_now()\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (now, now, page[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef unpublish_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (utc_now(), page[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef delete_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Permanently remove a Page. A Published one is remembered, so the\n    not-yet-Live count includes it until the next Export.\"\"\"\n    if page[\"status\"] == \"published\":\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO deleted_published (deleted_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page[\"id\"],))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef published_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Published Pages in Navigation order: nav_order, ties by title. No Author.\"\"\"\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT id, title, slug, body_md, nav_order FROM pages \"\n        \"WHERE status = 'published' ORDER BY nav_order, title COLLATE NOCASE, id\"\n    ).fetchall()\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\np='app/content.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''    last = conn.execute(\"SELECT max(ran_at) FROM exports\").fetchone()[0]\n    if last is None:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE status = 'published'\"\n                            ).fetchone()[0]\n    touched = conn.execute(\"SELECT count(*) FROM posts WHERE updated_at > ?\",\n                           (last,)).fetchone()[0]''','''    last = conn.execute(\"SELECT max(ran_at) FROM exports\").fetchone()[0]\n    if last is None:\n        return sum(conn.execute(f\"SELECT count(*) FROM {table} WHERE status = 'published'\"\n                                ).fetchone()[0] for table in (\"posts\", \"pages\"))\n    touched = sum(conn.execute(f\"SELECT count(*) FROM {table} WHERE updated_at > ?\",\n                               (last,)).fetchone()[0] for table in (\"posts\", \"pages\"))''')\ns=s.replace('\"\"\"Changes Members can\\'t see yet: Posts touched','\"\"\"Changes Members can\\'t see yet: Posts and Pages touched').replace(\"Live Posts deleted since then. {"parentUuid":"900bad96-8c6b-47c7-9cfb-9e0c28f0d5d9","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfhVUhYLwL8y9qUmEgY9p","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01F8VM81S61heF1NKyBzDjg3","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"sed -i \"s/path_for('pages')/path_for('page_list')/\" templates/admin/layout.html && cat > app/routes/pages.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Page list, editor and Preview. Admin only: the router-level check covers every route.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app import pages as store\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\n\nEDITOR_FIELDS = (\"title\", \"slug\", \"body_md\", \"nav_order\")\nBLANK = {\"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"0\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, page: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit page\" if page.get(\"id\") else \"New page\", \"user\": user,\n         \"page\": page, \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    page = store.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Page not found\")\n    return page\n\n\nasync def pending_values(request: Request) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"The editor's values as submitted, or None if the form carried none.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    if \"title\" not in form:\n        return None\n    return {name: str(form.get(name) or \"\") for name in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n\n\ndef clean(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row, values: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Validate the editor's values (everything but the body) without saving.\"\"\"\n    return store.clean_update(conn, page, **{k: v for k, v in values.items()\n                                             if k != \"body_md\"})\n\n\ndef changes(page: sqlite3.Row, cleaned: dict, body_md: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Plain-language list of what saving would change.\"\"\"\n    labels = {\"title\": \"Title\", \"slug\": \"Slug\", \"nav_order\": \"Navigation order\"}\n    lines = [f\"{label}: “{page[key]}” → “{cleaned[key]}”\"\n             for key, label in labels.items() if page[key] != cleaned[key]]\n    if page[\"body_md\"] != body_md:\n        lines.append(\"Body: changed (see the preview below)\")\n    return lines or [\"No changes.\"]\n\n\ndef article_context(page: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"What public/_article.html needs to show a Page as Members will see it.\"\"\"\n    return {\"post\": page, \"date_label\": None, \"kind_label\": \"\", \"event_label\": \"\",\n            \"body_html\": render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"])}\n\n\ndef confirmation(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, page: sqlite3.Row, *, action: str,\n                 heading: str, summary: list[str], pending: dict | None, button: str,\n                 preview: dict | None = None):\n    \"\"\"The Confirmation screen: nothing has been changed yet (see routes/posts.py).\"\"\"\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"summary\": summary, \"pending\": pending or {},\n         \"button\": button,\n         \"action_url\": request.app.url_path_for(action, page_id=page[\"id\"]),\n         \"cancel_url\": request.app.url_path_for(\"back_to_page_editor\", page_id=page[\"id\"]),\n         \"preview\": preview and article_context(preview)})\n\n\ndef editor_page(request, user, page: sqlite3.Row, values: dict | None, error: str):\n    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(page), **(values or {})}, error=error,\n                     status_code=422)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"user\": user, \"pages\": store.list_pages(conn)})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(BLANK))\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_page(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"),\n                body_md: str = Form(\"\"), nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    values = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, \"nav_order\": nav_order}\n    try:\n        page_id = store.create_page(conn, author_id=user[\"id\"], **values)\n    except store.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, values, error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef edit_page(request: Request, page_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, page_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef save_page(request: Request, page_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n              values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    values = values or dict(BLANK)\n    try:\n        cleaned = clean(conn, page, values)\n        if page[\"status\"] == \"published\" and not confirm:\n            return
